                \texttt{Player D: PLAN: move D from (R2,C6) to (R2,C1) — REQUEST: please clear (R2,C5), preferably down} \\
            }
        }
    }
    & & \\ \\

    \theutterance \stepcounter{utterance}  
    & & & \multicolumn{2}{p{0.3\linewidth}}{
        \cellcolor[rgb]{0.9,0.9,0.9}{
            \makecell[{{p{\linewidth}}}]{
                \texttt{\tiny{[GM$|$GM]}}
                \texttt{Board state repeated 3 times — players stuck in cycle} \\
            }
        }
    }
    & & \\ \\

\end{supertabular}
}
